# Graph Report - Reconciliation-Verification-Tool-for-Indian-CA-Firms  (2026-11-06)

## Corpus Check
- Large corpus: 1671 files · ~2,018,424 words. Semantic extraction will be expensive (many Claude tokens). Consider running on a subfolder.

## Summary
- 39225 nodes · 52803 edges · 3848 communities (3756 shown, 92 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2106 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output
